serif">Automating Merchant Onboarding at DoorDash</h1><p class="max-w-[640px] text-[16px] font-normal leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.02em] text-[#0e0e0a] text-pretty" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">How DoorDash trained a proprietary agent using internal experts and delivered a 30% relative reduction in critical menu errors</p></div><div class="mt-10 mx-auto w-full max-w-[1032px]"><div class="relative w-full overflow-hidden rounded-[7.67px]" style="height:258px"><svg viewBox="0 0 1032 258" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice" aria-hidden="true" class="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 h-full w-full"><style>
        /* ── Entrance bloom: scale from centre, peaks then settles to breathe start ── */
        @keyframes cs-bloom {
          0%   { opacity: 0;   transform: scale(0.2); }
          65%  { opacity: 0.8; transform: scale(1);   }
          100% { opacity: 0.1; transform: scale(1);   }
        }

        /* ── Continuous breathe — picks up seamlessly from bloom's end opacity ── */
        @keyframes cs-breathe {
          0%, 100% { opacity: 0.1; }
          50%       { opacity: 0.9; }
        }

        /* ── Content fade-up ── */
        @keyframes cs-content-in {
          from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(6px); }
          to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
        }
        .cs-content {
          animation: cs-content-in 0.7s ease-out 0.6s both;
        }

        .cs-ring {
          stroke-width: 1.6;
          transform-box: view-box;
          transform-origin: 516px 129px;
        }

        /* Innermost (d) blooms first → outermost (a) last */
        .cs-ring-d {
          animation:
            cs-bloom   1s ease-out 0s    1 both,
            cs-breathe 4s cubic-bezier(0.45, 0, 0.55, 1) 1s    infinite;
        }
        .cs-ring-c {
          animation:
            cs-bloom   1s ease-out 0.15s 1 both,
            cs-breathe 4s cubic-bezier(0.45, 0, 0.55, 1) 1.15s infinite;
        }
        .cs-ring-b {
          animation:
            cs-bloom   1s ease-out 0.30s 1 both,
            cs-breathe 4s cubic-bezier(0.45, 0, 0.55, 1) 1.30s infinite;
        }
        .cs-ring-a {
          animation:
            cs-bloom   1s ease-out 0.45s 1 both,
            cs-breathe 4s cubic-bezier(0.45, 0, 0.55, 1) 1.45s infinite;
        }

        @keyframes cs-dash {
          from { stroke-dashoffset: 0; }
          to   { stroke-dashoffset: -190; }
        }
        .cs-dash {
          fill: none;
          stroke: white;
          stroke-opacity: 0.3;
          stroke-width: 1.5;
          stroke-dasharray: 50 140;
        }
        .cs-dash-a { animation: cs-dash 9s linear infinite; animation-delay: -1s; }
        .cs-dash-b { animation: cs-dash 7s linear infinite; animation-delay: -4s; }
        .cs-dash-c { animation: cs-dash 5.5s linear infinite; animation-delay: -2s; }
        .cs-dash-d { animation: cs-dash 4s linear infinite; animation-delay: -3s; }

        @keyframes cs-dash-group-in {
          from { opacity: 0; }
          to   { opacity: 1; }
        }
        .cs-dash-group {
          animation: cs-dash-group-in 0.6s ease-out 1s both;
        }
      </style><g clip-path="url(#cs-clip)"><rect width="1032" height="258" fill="url(#cs-bg)"></rect><g class="cs-dash-group"><path class="cs-dash cs-dash-a" d="M-70 -48.5C91.7843 -48.5 238.234 helps merchants succeed. By encoding our quality standards directly into model training, we scaled our internal expertise and raised the bar on menu accuracy on the platform.&quot;</p><div class="flex items-center gap-2 class="text-label1-mobile md:text-label1-desktop text-foreground leading-none tracking-[-0.02em]">Andy Fang</span><div class="bg-primary size-1"></div><span class="text-label1-mobile md:text-label1-desktop text-foreground leading-none tracking-[-0.02em]">Co-Founder of DoorDash</span></div></blockquote><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">DoorDash is a global leader in food delivery, with a core focus on helping merchants run their businesses seamlessly. During onboarding, DoorDash enables merchants to go live in as little as one day by using AI to generate highly accurate menus. Menu accuracy is critical because it impacts whether orders run smoothly, restaurants prepare the right items, and customers get what they ordered.</p><div class="cs-wide w-full"><figure class="flex flex-col gap-2"><div class="bg-textured overflow-hidden rounded-[4px] border border-gray-100"><img alt="A joint effort between George Ignatius (DoorDash ML Engineer), Ying Yang (DoorDash Head of Merchant ML), and the Applied Compute team" draggable="false" loading="lazy" width="1530" height="862" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="h-auto w-full object-cover" style="color:transparent" class="text-label1-mobile md:text-label1-desktop text-center leading-tight tracking-[-0.02em] text-gray-400">A joint effort between George Ignatius (DoorDash ML Engineer), Ying Yang (DoorDash Head of Merchant ML), and the Applied Compute team</figcaption></figure></div><h3 id="understanding-the-problem" class="mt-7 first:mt-0 lg:mt-9 text-[26px] font-normal leading-[1.4] tracking-[-0.02em] text-article-dark-text font-tiempos w-full text-pretty">Understanding the Problem</h3><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">DoorDash had already built a sophisticated AI system that automatically converts photos, PDFs, and text menus into structured listings during merchant onboarding, enabling restaurants to get on the platform and start receiving orders immediately.</p><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">The team holds an extremely high accuracy bar for any menu created through their AI system, but the sheer diversity of real-world menus pushed the technology to its limit.</p><blockquote class="flex flex-col gap-8 rounded-[12px] px-8 py-16" style="background-color:var(--article-quote-bg)"><p class="text-[28px] font-[300] leading-[1.4] tracking-[-0.02em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-tiempos-headline), Georgia, serif">&quot;The existing AI automation system handled most of the menus well, but there was always a long tail of messy menus with hard-to-summarize styles. We relied on internal human experts, and recognized the impact of improvement efforts started to become marginal.” </p><div class="flex items-center gap-2 self-end"><img alt="Ying Yang" draggable="false" loading="lazy" width="256" height="256" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="size-6 rounded-full object-cover grayscale-100" style="color:transparent" sizes="24px" class="text-label1-mobile md:text-label1-desktop text-foreground leading-none tracking-[-0.02em]">Ying Yang</span><div class="bg-primary size-1"></div><span class="text-label1-mobile md:text-label1-desktop text-foreground leading-none tracking-[-0.02em]">DoorDash Head of Merchant ML</span></div></blockquote><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">DoorDash and Applied Compute realized that structured menu generation is hard to build a unified standard operating procedure for – even human experts had conflicting views on it. However, verification given a structured menu output and ground truth was much easier for human experts to agree upon. So the challenge then became building an automated grader that matches the way human experts do verification, which then could be trained against using reinforcement learning.</p><h3 id="calibrating-an-automated-grader" class="mt-7 first:mt-0 lg:mt-9 text-[26px] font-normal leading-[1.4] tracking-[-0.02em] text-article-dark-text font-tiempos w-full text-pretty">Calibrating an Automated Grader</h3><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">Building an extremely calibrated grader is critical in reinforcement learning, since the grader is what determines the rollouts that are positively or negatively reinforced. In particular, it is important for the grader to be (a) aligned with ground truth grading from human experts so that training against it gets closer to the desired state and (b) self-consistent so that the training process isn’t noisy and inefficient.</p><div class="cs-wide w-full"><figure class="flex flex-col gap-2 mx-auto max-w-[539px]"><div class="bg-textured overflow-hidden rounded-[4px] border border-gray-100"><img alt="Working onsite at DoorDash’s Sunnyvale office to translate production QA labels into an automated grader" draggable="false" loading="lazy" width="1078" height="610" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="h-auto w-full object-cover" style="color:transparent" sizes="(min-width: 1024px) 539px, 100vw" class="text-label1-mobile md:text-label1-desktop text-center leading-tight tracking-[-0.02em] text-gray-400">Working onsite at DoorDash’s Sunnyvale office to translate production QA labels into an automated grader</figcaption></figure></div><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">DoorDash’s high-quality quality assurance (QA) requirements, process, and human experts were instrumental in jumpstarting and then building the automated grader. Applied Compute’s tools helped quickly ladder-climb an effective grader setup that progressively became more aligned and self-consistent with the way human experts did verification.</p><blockquote class="flex flex-col gap-8 rounded-[12px] px-8 py-16" style="background-color:var(--article-quote-bg)"><p class="text-[28px] font-[300] leading-[1.4] tracking-[-0.02em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-tiempos-headline), Georgia, serif">“The grader encoded the same rules our reviewers use,  including the judgment-heavy edge cases. That gave us a reliable yardstick for reward function quality during model training.”</p><div class="flex items-center gap-2 self-end"><img alt="George Ignatius" draggable="false" loading="lazy" class="text-label1-mobile md:text-label1-desktop text-foreground leading-none tracking-[-0.02em]">George Ignatius</span><div class="bg-primary size-1"></div><span class="text-label1-mobile md:text-label1-desktop text-foreground leading-none tracking-[-0.02em]">DoorDash ML Engineer</span></div></blockquote><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">Applied Compute’s product stack, including experiment tracking, automated failure detection, and grader calibration, enabled rapid iteration and surfaced deep domain knowledge previously embedded only in the DoorDash team’s experience. The result was an automated grader that reliably captured DoorDash&#x27;s quality standards and could score model outputs automatically during training.</p><h3 id="training-a-model-against-doordashs-definition-of-quality" class="mt-7 first:mt-0 lg:mt-9 text-[26px] font-normal leading-[1.4] tracking-[-0.02em] text-article-dark-text font-tiempos w-full text-pretty">Training a Model Against DoorDash’s Definition of Quality</h3><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">Once the automated grader was reliable, it became the reward function for training a new menu error correction model to reduce menu error rate. Applied Compute&#x27;s system took DoorDash&#x27;s existing structured output and improved upon it, using reinforcement learning to maximize accuracy while meeting DoorDash&#x27;s latency, cost, and reliability constraints. Together, the companies instrumented the system to monitor model performance across different menu error types, and tested multiple base models and training recipes to meet production requirements.</p><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">During training, the automated grading system scored the model’s structured output and determined the different types of errors based on DoorDash’s menu style guidelines, ultimately converting the errors into a single reward signal and creating a feedback loop that drove reliable improvements.</p><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">Below is a very simple example from the model’s chain-of-thought as it learned more subtle, relevant thinking over the course of training about how to correctly structure menu hierarchies:</p><div class="bg-lightgrey text-body-mobile md:text-body-desktop overflow-x-auto rounded-[4px] border border-gray-100 px-4 py-4 font-mono leading-normal max-w-full"><div class="flex gap-4"><pre class="text-foreground min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-x-auto leading-normal whitespace-break-spaces"><code class="md:text-label1-desktop text-label1-mobile font-mono">So they are different: one is dinner (with rice, beans, salad) and the other is just tacos (no side).

We have two categories: one for &quot;Taco Dinner&quot; (with side) and one for &quot;Tacos&quot; (without side).</code></pre></div></div><h3 id="to-production-and-beyond" class="mt-7 first:mt-0 lg:mt-9 text-[26px] font-normal leading-[1.4] tracking-[-0.02em] text-article-dark-text font-tiempos w-full text-pretty">To Production and Beyond</h3><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">DoorDash had confidence in moving forward with production testing due to the strong RL training performance, consistent offline results, and tracked online metrics.</p><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">Once the model showed strong and stable automated results, the team moved to stricter offline validation with human graders reflecting production conditions. DoorDash then ran an A/B test against their baseline system on a large sample of production menus. Human reviewers graded both versions and confirmed the gains were real.</p><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">In the test, the share of low-quality menus fell by roughly 30% relative to the baseline.</p><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">With automated and human validation aligned, the work moved to production. Applied Compute delivered a production-ready library that integrated directly into DoorDash’s codebase while meeting latency, robustness, and security constraints.</p><p class="text-[17px] leading-[1.5] tracking-[-0.03em] text-article-body-text" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">DoorDash has rolled out the error correction model to all menu traffic in the USA. As a next step, continuous performance monitoring can be used to feed production corrections back into training, establishing a repeatable flywheel for DoorDash to improve systems by turning judgement into an automated training signal.</p></div><div class="mt-16 lg:hidden border-t border-black/10 pt-10"><div class="flex flex-col"><p class="mb-4 text-[13px] font-normal tracking-wide text-[#0e0e0a] uppercase leading-[20px] opacity-50" style="font-family:var(--font-geist-mono), ui-monospace, monospace">ABOUT THE COMPANY</p><div class="mb-4"><img src="https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/29848190cb6c0fbed19d7b9d346ad96287677604-129x15.svg" alt="Company logo" class="h-8 w-auto max-w-[150px] object-contain object-left" style="mix-blend-mode:multiply;filter:grayscale(1)" draggable="false"/></div><p class="mb-3 text-[15px] leading-[1.4] text-[#0e0e0a]" style="font-family:var(--font-sans), system-ui, sans-serif">DoorDash is a technology and logistics company that started with on-demand door-to-door delivery. It empowers local businesses to connect with people through their platform of diners and dashers.</p><a href="https://about.doordash.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="mb-4 block text-[15px] font-medium leading-[1.4] text-primary transition-opacity hover:opacity-70" style="font-family:var(--font-inter), system-ui, sans-serif">Visit Site ⌝</a><div class="mb-4"><p class="mb-2 transform: scale(1);   }\n        }\n\n        /* ── Continuous breathe — picks up seamlessly from bloom's end opacity ── */\n        @keyframes cs-breathe {\n          0%, 100% { opacity: 0.1; }\n          50%       { opacity: 0.9; }\n        }\n\n        /* ── Content fade-up ── */\n        @keyframes cs-content-in {\n          from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(6px); }\n          to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }\n        }\n        .cs-content {\n          animation: cs-content-in 0.7s ease-out 0.6s both;\n        }\n\n        .cs-ring {\n          stroke-width: 1.6;\n          transform-box: view-box;\n          transform-origin: 516px 129px;\n        }\n\n        /* Innermost (d) blooms first → outermost (a) last */\n        .cs-ring-d {\n          animation:\n            cs-bloom   1s ease-out 0s    1 both,\n            cs-breathe 4s cubic-bezier(0.45, 0, 0.55, 1) 1s    infinite;\n        }\n        .cs-ring-c {\n          animation:\n            cs-bloom   1s ease-out 0.15s 1 both,\n            cs-breathe 4s cubic-bezier(0.45, 0, 0.55, 1) 1.15s infinite;\n        }\n        .cs-ring-b {\n          animation:\n            cs-bloom   1s ease-out 0.30s 1 both,\n            cs-breathe 4s cubic-bezier(0.45, 0, 0.55, 1) 1.30s infinite;\n        }\n        .cs-ring-a {\n          animation:\n            cs-bloom   1s ease-out 0.45s 1 both,\n            cs-breathe 4s cubic-bezier(0.45, 0, 0.55, 1) 1.45s infinite;\n        }\n\n        @keyframes cs-dash {\n          from { stroke-dashoffset: 0; }\n          to   { stroke-dashoffset: -190; }\n        }\n        .cs-dash {\n          fill: none;\n          stroke: white;\n          stroke-opacity: 0.3;\n          stroke-width: 1.5;\n          stroke-dasharray: 50 140;\n        }\n        .cs-dash-a { animation: cs-dash 9s linear infinite; animation-delay: -1s; }\n        .cs-dash-b { animation: cs-dash 7s linear infinite; animation-delay: -4s; }\n        .cs-dash-c { animation: cs-dash 5.5s lg:px-12 lg:py-24\",\"children\":[\"$\",\"div\",null,{\"className\":\"grid grid-cols-1 gap-x-10 gap-y-0 lg:grid-cols-[240px_minmax(0,1fr)]\",\"children\":[[\"$\",\"aside\",null,{\"className\":\"hidden lg:block w-[240px]\",\"children\":[\"$\",\"$L51\",null,{\"children\":[[\"$\",\"$L52\",null,{\"headings\":[{\"id\":\"understanding-the-problem\",\"text\":\"Understanding the Problem\",\"level\":3},{\"id\":\"calibrating-an-automated-grader\",\"text\":\"Calibrating an Automated Grader\",\"level\":3},{\"id\":\"training-a-model-against-doordashs-definition-of-quality\",\"text\":\"Training a Model Against DoorDash’s Definition of Quality\",\"level\":3},{\"id\":\"to-production-and-beyond\",\"text\":\"To Production and Beyond\",\"level\":3}]}],[\"$\",\"$L53\",null,{\"companyLogo\":{\"asset\":{\"_id\":\"image-29848190cb6c0fbed19d7b9d346ad96287677604-129x15-svg\",\"metadata\":{\"dimensions\":{\"height\":15,\"width\":129}},\"url\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/29848190cb6c0fbed19d7b9d346ad96287677604-129x15.svg\"}},\"companyDescription\":\"DoorDash is a technology and logistics company that started with on-demand door-to-door delivery. It empowers local businesses to connect with people through their platform of diners and dashers.\",\"companyWebsite\":\"https://about.doordash.com/\",\"industry\":null,\"champions\":[{\"image\":{\"asset\":{\"_id\":\"image-3717d120635ff108418d49b0bb2bce5b0ce4f5bb-200x200-jpg\",\"metadata\":{\"dimensions\":{\"height\":200,\"width\":200}},\"url\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/3717d120635ff108418d49b0bb2bce5b0ce4f5bb-200x200.jpg\"}},\"name\":\"Ying Yang\"},{\"image\":{\"asset\":{\"_id\":\"image-0cb04c9863b94aca5ec9634a3638d7ff182280f9-48x48-webp\",\"metadata\":{\"dimensions\":{\"height\":48,\"width\":48}},\"url\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/0cb04c9863b94aca5ec9634a3638d7ff182280f9-48x48.webp\"}},\"name\":\"George Ignatius\"}]}]]}]}],[\"$\",\"article\",null,{\"className\":\"min-w-0\",\"children\":[[\"$\",\"$L54\",null,{\"value\":[{\"_key\":\"f61ddd7e8e51\",\"_type\":\"bigQuote\",\"author\":\"Andy Fang\",\"image\":{\"_type\":\"image\",\"asset\":{\"_ref\":\"image-c8aa413775f23f3f400b5c2a3965535bb4fd78c6-1024x1024-png\",\"_type\":\"reference\"}},\"role\":\"Co-Founder of DoorDash\",\"text\":\"\\\"AI has long been core to how DoorDash helps merchants succeed. By encoding our quality standards directly into model training, we scaled our internal expertise and raised the bar on menu accuracy on the platform.\\\"\"},{\"_key\":\"fc840be74950\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"bf48294dcd8a\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"DoorDash is a global leader in food delivery, with a core focus on helping merchants run their businesses seamlessly. During onboarding, DoorDash enables merchants to go live in as little as one day by using AI to generate highly accurate menus. Menu accuracy is critical because it impacts whether orders run smoothly, restaurants prepare the right items, and customers get what they ordered.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"4c1c2d0836e8\",\"_type\":\"captionedImage\",\"caption\":\"A joint effort between George Ignatius (DoorDash ML Engineer), Ying Yang (DoorDash Head of Merchant ML), and the Applied Compute team\",\"dimensions\":{\"_type\":\"sanity.imageDimensions\",\"aspectRatio\":1.7749419953596288,\"height\":862,\"width\":1530},\"image\":{\"_type\":\"image\",\"asset\":{\"_ref\":\"image-9913f4d866fdc8cecc5606b2bd061f2976d12660-1530x862-png\",\"_type\":\"reference\"}},\"imageUrl\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/9913f4d866fdc8cecc5606b2bd061f2976d12660-1530x862.png\",\"size\":\"full\"},{\"_key\":\"df9fbc91dea9\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"cdd01bb36bfc\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Understanding the Problem\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"h3\"},{\"_key\":\"30260de13aaf\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"3f19ec7ca03f\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"DoorDash had already built a sophisticated AI system that automatically converts photos, PDFs, and text menus into structured listings during merchant onboarding, enabling restaurants to get on the platform and start receiving orders immediately.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"1e712a23b261\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"c59ba8c45b6f\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"The team holds an extremely high accuracy bar for any menu created through their AI system, but the sheer diversity of real-world menus pushed the technology to its limit.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"bbf970622c78\",\"_type\":\"bigQuote\",\"author\":\"Ying Yang\",\"image\":{\"_type\":\"image\",\"asset\":{\"_ref\":\"image-61f85e87e683c4e62efc3bc1ef55a72c904ce5e9-1024x1024-png\",\"_type\":\"reference\"}},\"role\":\"DoorDash Head of Merchant ML\",\"text\":\"\\\"The existing AI automation system handled most of the menus well, but there was always a long tail of messy menus with hard-to-summarize styles. We relied on internal human experts, and recognized the impact of improvement efforts started to become marginal.” \"},{\"_key\":\"e517d3b9c89b\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"7229db2f9239\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"DoorDash and Applied Compute realized that structured menu generation is hard to build a unified standard operating procedure for – even human experts had conflicting views on it. However, verification given a structured menu output and ground truth was much easier for human experts to agree upon. So the challenge then became building an automated grader that matches the way human experts do verification, which then could be trained against using reinforcement learning.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"ca732849b2b4\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"18bdcfa719cd\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Calibrating an Automated Grader\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"h3\"},{\"_key\":\"84ca59352ad2\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"b05323b926ef\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Building an extremely calibrated grader is critical in reinforcement learning, since the grader is what determines the rollouts that are positively or negatively reinforced. In particular, it is important for the grader to be (a) aligned with ground truth grading from human experts so that training against it gets closer to the desired state and (b) self-consistent so that the training process isn’t noisy and inefficient.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"2aab22b682e9\",\"_type\":\"captionedImage\",\"caption\":\"Working onsite at DoorDash’s Sunnyvale office to translate production QA labels into an automated grader\",\"dimensions\":{\"_type\":\"sanity.imageDimensions\",\"aspectRatio\":1.7672131147540984,\"height\":610,\"width\":1078},\"image\":{\"_type\":\"image\",\"asset\":{\"_ref\":\"image-ab86859836fc2e06e0834a9a888fa297df0cda97-1078x610-png\",\"_type\":\"reference\"}},\"imageUrl\":\"https://cdn.sanity.io/images/rda7lbmb/production/ab86859836fc2e06e0834a9a888fa297df0cda97-1078x610.png\",\"size\":\"small\"},{\"_key\":\"be0da873f5ad\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"30d8bf67316b\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"DoorDash’s high-quality quality assurance (QA) requirements, process, and human experts were instrumental in jumpstarting and then building the automated grader. Applied Compute’s tools helped quickly ladder-climb an effective grader setup that progressively became more aligned and self-consistent with the way human experts did verification.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"648f28e7296f\",\"_type\":\"bigQuote\",\"author\":\"George Ignatius\",\"image\":{\"_type\":\"image\",\"asset\":{\"_ref\":\"image-b3c55d89e021bc636d38e41c4fbd8d6bbce48f12-1024x1024-png\",\"_type\":\"reference\"}},\"role\":\"DoorDash ML Engineer\",\"text\":\"“The grader encoded the same rules our reviewers use,  including the judgment-heavy edge cases. That gave us a reliable yardstick for reward function quality during model training.”\"},{\"_key\":\"b83a4066f7c4\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"4460a3c55862\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Applied Compute’s product stack, including experiment tracking, automated failure detection, and grader calibration, enabled rapid iteration and surfaced deep domain knowledge previously embedded only in the DoorDash team’s experience. The result was an automated grader that reliably captured DoorDash's quality standards and could score model outputs automatically during training.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"d6118b5fca58\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"5cc0da1cf0e4\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Training a Model Against DoorDash’s Definition of Quality\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"h3\"},{\"_key\":\"c01e92f3a98b\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"805ff852c35b\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Once the automated grader was reliable, it became the reward function for training a new menu error correction model to reduce menu error rate. Applied Compute's system took DoorDash's existing structured output and improved upon it, using reinforcement learning to maximize accuracy while meeting DoorDash's latency, cost, and reliability constraints. Together, the companies instrumented the system to monitor model performance across different menu error types, and tested multiple base models and training recipes to meet production requirements.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"e5be83cd39b4\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"2f0c38398207\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"During training, the automated grading system scored the model’s structured output and determined the different types of errors based on DoorDash’s menu style guidelines, ultimately converting the errors into a single reward signal and creating a feedback loop that drove reliable improvements.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"1c558aae88a5\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"2e59a312fc4f\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Below is a very simple example from the model’s chain-of-thought as it learned more subtle, relevant thinking over the course of training about how to correctly structure menu hierarchies:\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"eb8bf7377bef\",\"_type\":\"codeBlock\",\"code\":\"So they are different: one is dinner (with rice, beans, salad) and the other is just tacos (no side).\\n\\nWe have two categories: one for \\\"Taco Dinner\\\" (with side) and one for \\\"Tacos\\\" (without side).\",\"language\":\"text\",\"showLineNumbers\":false},{\"_key\":\"99b6ff57c08d\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"868b28f94d3c\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"To Production and Beyond\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"h3\"},{\"_key\":\"4bef0c6ed0fc\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"d459fc9cf849\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"DoorDash had confidence in moving forward with production testing due to the strong RL training performance, consistent offline results, and tracked online metrics.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"c440103865e7\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"fed1409bfdb0\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"Once the model showed strong and stable automated results, the team moved to stricter offline validation with human graders reflecting production conditions. DoorDash then ran an A/B test against their baseline system on a large sample of production menus. Human reviewers graded both versions and confirmed the gains were real.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"0cee3783af9f\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"2399797344dd\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"In the test, the share of low-quality menus fell by roughly 30% relative to the baseline.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"1ac5a7ed233b\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"c27ef816fa5f\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"With automated and human validation aligned, the work moved to production. Applied Compute delivered a production-ready library that integrated directly into DoorDash’s codebase while meeting latency, robustness, and security constraints.\"}],\"markDefs\":[],\"style\":\"normal\"},{\"_key\":\"0119b9ae3afb\",\"_type\":\"block\",\"children\":[{\"_key\":\"c27ef816fa5f\",\"_type\":\"span\",\"marks\":[],\"text\":\"DoorDash has rolled out the error correction model to all menu traffic in the USA. As a next step, continuous performance monitoring can be used to feed production corrections back into training, establishing a repeatable flywheel for DoorDash to improve systems by turning judgement into an automated training
